  controls.innerHTML=slider('rate','Learning rate Â· Î·',.001,.1,.001,.01)+`<div class="button-row"><button class="primary" id="one">Dar un paso</button><button class="secondary" id="ten">10 pasos</button><button class="secondary" id="reset">Reiniciar</button></div><p class="caption">Full batch sobre 8 puntos.   const advance=n=>{for(let i=0;i<n;i++){const [na,nb]=step(demoPoints,a,b,value('rate'));const loss=mse(demoPoints,na,nb);if(!Number.isFinite(loss)||loss>1e12){message='Divergencia: se detuvo el cÃ¡lculo antes de exceder el lÃ­mite numÃ©rico.  }else if(id==='matrix'){

ds=a.map((v,i)=>Math.abs(v-b[i]));\n  if(p===Infinity)return Math.max(...ds);\n  return ds.reduce((s,v)=>s+v**p,0)**(1/p);\n}\nexport function neighbors(points,q,k,p=2){return points.map((v,i)=>({...v,i,d:distance(v.x,q,p)})).sort((a,b)=>a.d-b.d||a.i-b.i).slice(0,k);}\nexport function predict(points,q,k,p=2,regression=false,weighted=false){\n  let ns=neighbors(points,q,k,p);\n  if(weighted&&ns.some(n=>n.d===0))ns=ns.filter(n=>n.d===0);\n  const weight=n=>weighted&&n.d>0?1/n.d:1;\n  if(regression)return ns.reduce((s,n)=>s+n.y*weight(n),0)/ns.reduce((s,n)=>s+weight(n),0);\n  const votes={};ns.forEach(n=>votes[n.y]=(votes[n.y]||0)+weight(n));\n  return Number(Object.keys(votes).sort((a,b)=>votes[b]-votes[a]||Number(a)-Number(b))[0]);\n}\nexport function mse(points,a,b){return points.reduce((s,v)=>s+(a*v.x+b-v.y)**2,0)/points.length;}\nexport function gradient(points,a,b){const n=points.length;return [2/n*points.reduce((s,v)=>s+(a*v.x+b-v.y)*v.x,0),2/n*points.reduce((s,v)=>s+a*v.x+b-v.y,0)];}\nexport function step(points,a,b,rate){const [ga,gb]=gradient(points,a,b);return [a-rate*ga,b-rate*gb];}\nexport function r2(points,a,b){const mean=points.reduce((s,v)=>s+v.y,0)/points.length;const total=points.reduce((s,v)=>s+(v.y-mean)**2,0);return total===0?null:1-points.length*mse(points,a,b)/total;}\nexport const shellFraction=(ratio,d)=>1-ratio**d;\nexport const sigmoid=z=>1/(1+Math.exp(-z));\nexport function logLoss(y,p){const q=Math.min(1-1e-15,Math.max(1e-15,p));return -(y*Math.log(q)+(1-y)*Math.log(1-q));}\nexport function perceptronUpdate(w,b,x,y,rate=1){const z=w.reduce((s,v,i)=>s+v*x[i],b),prediction=z>=0?1:0,error=prediction-y;return {z,prediction,error,w:w.map((v,i)=>v-rate*error*x[i]),b:b-rate*error};}\nexport function regularizedObjective(w,lambda,type='l2'){const data=(w-3)**2,penalty=lambda*(type==='l1'?Math.abs(w):w*w);return {data,penalty,total:data+penalty};}\n\r\n\nPART 5\nimport test from 'node:test';\nimport assert from 'node:assert/strict';\nimport {distance,neighbors,predict,mse,gradient,step,r2,shellFraction,sigmoid,logLoss,perceptronUpdate,regularizedObjective} from '../src/math.js';\nimport {lessons,quizzes,sources,weeks} from '../src/content.js';\nimport katex from 'katex';\nconst close=(a,b)=>assert.ok(Math.abs(a-b)<1e-9,`${a} != ${b}`);\ntest('Distances reproduce the 3-4-5 triangle and L infinity',()=>{assert.equal(distance([0,0],[3,4],1),7);assert.equal(distance([0,0],[3,4]),5);assert.equal(distance([0,0],[3,4],Infinity),4);});\ntest('kNN uses strict majority; k=1 never returns a class with zero votes',()=>{const data=[0,0,1,0,1].map((y,i)=>({x:[i],y}));assert.equal(predict(data,[0],5),0);assert.equal(predict(data,[0],1),0);});\ntest('kNN exact coincidences, vote ties and regression',()=>{const data=[{x:[0],y:0},{x:[0],y:1},{x:[2],y:1}];assert.equal(predict(data,[0],3,2,false,true),0);close(predict(data,[0],3,2,true,true),.5);close(predict([{x:[0],y:8},{x:[1],y:10},{x:[2],y:12}],[1],3,2,true),10);assert.deepEqual(neighbors(data,[0],2).map(n=>n.i),[0,1]);});\ntest('Regression MSE and R2 have hand-calculated results',()=>{const data=[{x:0,y:1},{x:1,y:3},{x:2,y:5}];close(mse(data,1,2),2/3);close(r2(data,1,2),.75);close(r2(data,0,10),-18.375);assert.equal(r2([{x:0,y:1},{x:1,y:1}],0,1),null);});\ntest('Analytic gradient matches finite differences; update is simultaneous',()=>{const data=[{x:0,y:1},{x:1,y:3}];assert.deepEqual(gradient(data,0,0),[-3,-4]);const [a,b]=step(data,0,0,.1);close(a,.3);close(b,.4);close(mse(data,a,b),2.825);const h=1e-5,g=gradient(data,.6,.2);for(let i=0;i<2;i++){const plus=i===0?mse(data,.6+h,.2):mse(data,.6,.2+h);const minus=i===0?mse(data,.6-h,.2):mse(data,.6,.2-h);close((plus-minus)/(2*h),g[i]);}});\ntest('High dimension shell uses volume exponent',()=>{close(shellFraction(.9,10),1-.3486784401);close(shellFraction(.5,3),.875);});\ntest('Logistic probability and log-loss match hand calculations',()=>{close(sigmoid(0),.5);close(sigmoid(Math.log(4)),.8);close(logLoss(1,.8),-Math.log(.8));close(logLoss(0,.2),-Math.log(.8));});\ntest('Perceptron update follows the course rule',()=>{assert.deepEqual(perceptronUpdate([0,0],0,[1,2],0,1),{z:0,prediction:1,error:1,w:[-1,-2],b:-1});});\ntest('Regularization separates data loss, penalty and total',()=>{assert.deepEqual(regularizedObjective(2,.5,'l2'),{data:1,penalty:2,total:3});assert.deepEqual(regularizedObjective(2,.5,'l1'),{data:1,penalty:1,total:2});});\ntest('All lessons have valid #!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail
# Run on the existing VPS as root. Only this site's paths are changed.
archive=${1:?archive path required}
expected=${2:?sha256 required}
release_id=${3:?release id required}
[[ "$archive" == /tmp/ds675-*.tgz && "$release_id" =~ ^ds675-[0-9-]+$ ]] || exit 2
printf '%s  %s\n' "$expected" "$archive" | sha256sum -c -
domain=ds675.automationfreelancer.com
release="/home/automationfreelancer/releases/$release_id"
site="/home/automationfreelancer/htdocs/$domain"
config="/etc/nginx/sites-enabled/$domain.conf"
if [[ -e "$site" && ! -L "$site" ]]; then echo 'Existing site is not a symlink; inspect before replacing.' >&2; exit 2; fi
[[ ! -e "$release" ]] || { echo 'Release already exists' >&2; exit 2; }
mkdir -p "$release"
tar -xzf "$archive" -C "$release"
test -f "$release/index.html"
test -f "$release/health.json"
chown -R automationfreelancer:automationfreelancer "$release"
find "$release" -type d -exec chmod 755 {} +
find "$release" -type f -exec chmod 644 {} +
previous=$(readlink "$site" || true)
mkdir -p /home/automationfreelancer/backups
backup="/home/automationfreelancer/backups/$release_id.nginx.conf"
[[ ! -f "$config" ]] || cp "$config" "$backup"
ln -s "$release" "$site.next"
chown -h automationfreelancer:automationfreelancer "$site.next"
mv -Tf "$site.next" "$site"
if [[ ! -f "/etc/letsencrypt/live/$domain/fullchain.pem" ]]; then
cat > "$config" <<'NGINX'
server {
 listen 80;
 listen [::]:80;
 server_name ds675.automationfreelancer.com;
 root /home/automationfreelancer/htdocs/ds675.automationfreelancer.com;
 index index.html;
 location ^~ /.well-known/acme-challenge/ { allow all; }
 location / { try_files $uri $uri/ =404; }
}
NGINX
nginx -t
systemctl reload nginx
 certbot certonly --webroot -w "$site" -d "$domain" --non-interactive --keep-until-expiring
fi
cat > "$config" <<'NGINX'
server {
 listen 80;
 listen [::]:80;
 server_name ds675.automationfreelancer.com;
 root /home/automationfreelancer/htdocs/ds675.automationfreelancer.com;
 location ^~ /.well-known/acme-challenge/ { allow all; }
 location / { return 301 https://$host$request_uri; }
}
server {
 listen 443 ssl;
 listen [::]:443 ssl;
 server_name ds675.automationfreelancer.com;
 ssl_certificate /etc/letsencrypt/live/ds675.automationfreelancer.com/fullchain.pem;

 include /etc/letsencrypt/options-ssl-nginx.conf;

 root /home/automationfreelancer/htdocs/ds675.automationfreelancer.com;
 index index.html;

Elastic net combina ambas.',\r\n'L_{reg}=L+\\\\lambda_1\\\\lVert w\\\\rVert_1+\\\\lambda_2\\\\lVert w\\\\rVert_2^2',\r\n'L es la pérdida de datos; λ₁ y λ₂ controlan las penalizaciones; ‖w‖₁ suma valores absolutos y ‖w‖₂² suma cuadrados.',\r\n['Sin penalización, el optimizador solo busca reducir el error del entrenamiento.','Al aumentar λ, un peso grande añade más costo y el óptimo se mueve hacia cero.','En scikit-learn, C es inverso a la fuerza de regularización: C grande implica penalización más débil.'],\r\n`from sklearn.linear_model import LogisticRegression\r\nl2 = LogisticRegression(penalty=\"l2\", C=1.0, max_iter=1000)\r\nl1 = LogisticRegression(penalty=\"l1\", C=1.0,\r\n                        solver=\"liblinear\", max_iter=1000)\r\nl2.fit(X_train, y_train); l1.fit(X_train, y_train)`,\r\n'La compatibilidad entre penalty y solver importa. C debe seleccionarse con validación, no mirando el test final repetidamente.',\r\n['Creer que regularización corrige automáticamente etiquetas equivocadas.','Interpretar coeficiente cero como prueba causal de irrelevancia.','Comparar magnitudes de pesos sin escalar variables.'],\r\n'Si C pasa de 1 a 100, ¿la regularización se hace más fuerte o más débil?',\r\n'Más débil, porque scikit-learn usa C como inverso de la intensidad de regularización.', ['perceptron-limits','regularization-geometry','logistic-loss'],'regularization'),\r\nlesson('regularization-geometry',4,6,'La geometría de L1 y L2','Contornos, compromiso y selección de características',18,[['w4reg','Video 3 · Geometric Understanding']],\r\n'La pérdida de datos tiene contornos alrededor de su mínimo. La penalización tira de la solución hacia el origen. El nuevo mínimo es un compromiso entre ambos objetivos.',\r\n'Los contornos L2 son redondeados; los de L1 tienen esquinas sobre los ejes. Esas esquinas hacen frecuente que el contacto ocurra con alguna coordenada exactamente en cero.',\r\n'\\\\lVert w\\\\rVert_2^2=w_1^2+w_2^2,\\\\qquad \\\\lVert w\\\\rVert_1=|w_1|+|w_2|',\r\n'L2 forma círculos de nivel en dos dimensiones; L1 forma rombos. Ambas penalizaciones son convexas; L1 no es diferenciable en cero.',\r\n['Imagina el mínimo sin regularización lejos del origen.','Al añadir L2, el óptimo se desplaza suavemente y normalmente conserva pesos pequeños no nulos.','Con L1, una esquina del rombo puede tocar un contorno de la pérdida sobre un eje: el peso correspondiente queda en cero.'],\r\n`from sklearn.linear_model import LogisticRegression\r\nfor penalty, solver in [(\"l2\",\"lbfgs\"),(\"l1\",\"liblinear\")]:\r\n    model = LogisticRegression(penalty=penalty, solver=solver,\r\n                               C=.2, max_iter=1000)\r\n    model.fit(X_train, y_train)\r\n    print(penalty, model.coef_)`,\r\n'El ejemplo compara coeficientes bajo una regularización relativamente fuerte. Los resultados dependen de los datos y del escalado.',\r\n['Decir que L1 no es convexa; sí lo es, aunque no sea diferenciable en cero.','Esperar que L2 produzca muchos ceros exactos.','Elegir L1 solo porque genera menos variables sin validar desempeño y estabilidad.'],\r\n'¿Por qué L1 puede funcionar como selección de características?',\r\n'Porque su geometría favorece soluciones en los ejes, donde algunos coeficientes quedan exactamente en cero.', ['regularization','features','logistic-sklearn'],'regularization')\r\n];\r\nexport const weeks=[\r\n {id:1,title:'De los datos a los vecinos',subtitle:'Fundamentos · Module 1 + Module 2',description:'Representa datos, identifica la tarea y construye tu primera intuición de cómo aprende un modelo.',objectives:['Distinguir clasificación y regresión.','Leer X, y y las dimensiones de una operación.','Calcular distancias y explicar un voto kNN.','Distinguir memorizar de generalizar.'],summary:'Los ejemplos etiquetados se convierten en vectores. const slider=(id,label,min,max,step,value)=>`<label class="control" for="${id}"><span>${label} <output id="${id}-value">${value}</output></span><input id="${id}" type="range" min="${min}" max="${max}" step="${step}" value="${value}"></label>`;
const select=(id,label,opts)=>`<label class="control" for="${id}"><span>${label}</span><select id="${id}">${opts.map(([v,t])=>`<option value="${v}">${t}</option>`).join('')}</select></label>`;
const svg=(body,label)=>`<svg viewBox="0 0 600 360" role="img" aria-label="${label}">${body}</svg>`;
const plot=(body,label)=>svg(`<rect x="45" y="20" width="525" height="290" fill="var(--plot)" rx="8"/>${[0,1,2,3,4,5].map(i=>`<path d="M45 ${20+i*58}H570 M${45+i*105} 20V310" stroke="var(--line)" stroke-width="1"/>`).join('')}<path d="M45 20V310H570" fill="none" stroke="var(--muted)"/><text x="300" y="350" text-anchor="middle">x · característica</text><text x="15" y="180" transform="rotate(-90 15 180)" text-anchor="middle">y · respuesta / segunda característica</text>${body}`,label);
const sx=x=>45+x*52.5, sy=y=>310-y*29;
const dot=(x,y,color='#127e80',r=5)=>`<circle cx="${sx(x)}" cy="${sy(y)}" r="${r}" fill="${color}" stroke="var(--surface)" stroke-width="2"/>`;
const stat=(label,value)=>`<div class="lab-stat"><small>${label}</small><strong>${value}</strong></div>`;
const table=(heads,rows)=>`<div class="table-scroll"><table><thead><tr>${heads.map(x=>`<th>${x}</th>`).join('')}</tr></thead><tbody>${rows.map(r=>`<tr>${r.map(x=>`<td>${x}</td>`).join('')}</tr>`).join('')}</tbody></table></div>`;
const demoPoints=[{x:1,y:2},{x:2,y:2.8},{x:3,y:4.1},{x:4,y:4.6},{x:5,y:6.2},{x:6,y:6.6},{x:7,y:8.1},{x:8,y:8.6}];
export function mountLab(el,id){
 const item=labs.find(l=>l[0]===id)||labs[0];id=item[0];
 el.innerHTML=`<div class="lab-shell"><div class="lab-controls"><span class="eyebrow">LABORATORIO INTERACTIVO</span><h3>${item[1]}</h3><p>${item[2]}</p><div id="controls"></div><p class="caption">Ejemplo didáctico adicional. Los cálculos se ejecutan en tu navegador.</p></div><div class="lab-output" id="lab-output"></div></div>`;
 const controls=el.querySelector('#controls'),out=el.querySelector('#lab-output');const value=id=>Number(controls.querySelector('#'+id).value);let draw;
 if(id==='knn'){
  const points=[[1,2,0],[2,3,0],[2,1,0],[3,4,0],[4,2,0],[4.2,5,1],[5,7,0],[6,5,1],[6,8,1],[7,6,1],[8,8,1],[9,7,1],[7,3,1],[3,7,0],[5,4,1]].map(([x,y,c])=>({x:[x,y],y:c}));
  controls.innerHTML=slider('k','Vecinos · k',1,15,1,5)+select('metric','Distancia',[['2','L₂ · Euclídea'],['1','L₁ · Manhattan'],['Infinity','L∞ · Chebyshev']])+select('mode','Tarea',[['0','Clasificación'],['1','Regresión']])+select('weights','Ponderación',[['0','Uniforme'],['1','Inversa de distancia']])+slider('qx','Consulta · x₁',0,10,.1,5)+slider('qy','Consulta · x₂',0,10,.1,5);
  draw=()=>{const k=value('k'),p=value('metric'),q=[value('qx'),value('qy')],reg=!!value('mode'),weighted=!!value('weights');const data=points.map(v=>({...v,y:reg?2*v.x[0]+v.x[1]:v.y}));const ns=neighbors(data,q,k,p);const pred=predict(data,q,k,p,reg,weighted);let regions='';if(!reg)for(let x=0;x<10;x+=.4)for(let y=0;y<10;y+=.4){const c=predict(data,[x+.2,y+.2],k,p,false,weighted);regions+=`<rect x="${sx(x)}" y="${sy(y+.4)}" width="21.1" height="11.7" fill="${c?'#e5cfed':'#bce3df'}" opacity=".65"/>`;}
   out.innerHTML=plot(regions+ns.map(n=>`<path d="M${sx(q[0])} ${sy(q[1])}L${sx(n.x[0])} ${sy(n.x[1])}" stroke="#526d73" stroke-dasharray="4 4"/>`).join('')+data.map((n,i)=>dot(n.x[0],n.x[1],reg?'#127e80':n.y?'#9c68b4':'#127e80',ns.some(v=>v.i===i)?7:4)).join('')+`<path d="M${sx(q[0])-8} ${sy(q[1])}h16 M${sx(q[0])} ${sy(q[1])-8}v16" stroke="#e36443" stroke-width="4"/>`,'Vecinos de la consulta, cruz naranja, y regiones de clasificación')+`<div class="lab-stats">${stat('Predicción',reg?fmt(pred):'Clase '+pred)}${stat('Vecinos',k)}${stat('Distancia',p===Infinity?'L∞':'L'+p)}</div><p class="caption">Verde: clase 0 · Violeta: clase 1 · Cruz naranja: consulta. ${reg?'Targets sintéticos: y = 2x₁ + x₂.':'El fondo muestra la clase predicha. Los puntos grandes son los vecinos seleccionados.'} Empates de voto: clase menor; de distancia: orden de los puntos.</p>`+table(['Vecino','Distancia','Target'],ns.map(n=>['('+n.x.join(', ')+')',fmt(n.d),n.y]));};
 }else if(id==='distances'){
  controls.innerHTML=select('p','Norma',[['1','L₁ · rombo'],['2','L₂ · círculo'],['4','L₄'],['Infinity','L∞ · cuadrado']])+slider('dx','Diferencia |Δx₁|',0,5,.1,3)+slider('dy','Diferencia |Δx₂|',0,5,.1,4)+slider('sigma','σ · similitud gaussiana',.1,5,.1,2);
  draw=()=>{const p=value('p'),x=value('dx'),y=value('dy'),sigma=value('sigma');let pts=[];for(let t=0;t<=Math.PI*2+.01;t+=.025){let a=Math.cos(t),b=Math.sin(t),norm=p===Infinity?Math.max(Math.abs(a),Math.abs(b)):(Math.abs(a)**p+Math.abs(b)**p)**(1/p);pts.push(`${300+120*a/norm},${170-120*b/norm}`);}out.innerHTML=svg(`<path d="M100 170H500 M300 15V325" stroke="var(--muted)"/><polyline points="${pts.join(' ')}" fill="#bce3df88" stroke="#127e80" stroke-width="3"/><text x="430" y="192">1</text><text x="310" y="45">1</text><text x="300" y="352" text-anchor="middle">Frontera a distancia 1 del origen · L${p===Infinity?'∞':p}</text>`,'Bola unitaria de la norma seleccionada')+`<div class="lab-stats">${stat('L₁',fmt(x+y))}${stat('L₂',fmt(Math.hypot(x,y)))}${stat('L∞',fmt(Math.max(x,y)))}</div><p>Para Δ=(${x}, ${y}), L₂ = √(${fmt(x*x)} + ${fmt(y*y)}).</p><p>Similitud gaussiana: exp(−${fmt(x*x+y*y)}/(2·${sigma}²)) = <strong>${fmt(Math.exp(-(x*x+y*y)/(2*sigma*sigma)))}</strong>.</p><p class="caption">La curva representa la bola unitaria; los controles Δ calculan un ejemplo separado. La similitud usa L₂ independientemente de la norma dibujada.</p>`;};
 }else if(id==='regression'){
  controls.innerHTML=slider('a','Pendiente · a',-.5,2,.05,.6)+slider('b','Intercepto · b',-2,4,.1,1)+select('outlier','Observación atípica',[['0','Sin outlier'],['1','Cambiar último target a 2']]);
  draw=()=>{const a=value('a'),b=value('b');const pts=demoPoints.map((v,i)=>({...v,y:value('outlier')&&i===7?2:v.y}));out.innerHTML=plot(`<defs><clipPath id="graph-clip"><rect x="45" y="20" width="525" height="290"/></clipPath></defs><g clip-path="url(#graph-clip)">${pts.map(v=>`<path d="M${sx(v.x)} ${sy(v.y)}V${sy(a*v.x+b)}" stroke="#df7859" stroke-dasharray="4 3"/>`).join('')}<path d="M${sx(0)} ${sy(b)}L${sx(10)} ${sy(10*a+b)}" stroke="#127e80" stroke-width="3"/>${pts.map(v=>dot(v.x,v.y)).join('')}</g>`,'Recta y residuos verticales')+`<div class="lab-stats">${stat('MSE',fmt(mse(pts,a,b)))}${stat('R²',fmt(r2(pts,a,b)))}${stat('Modelo',a+'x + '+b)}</div>`+table(['x','y real','ŷ','(ŷ − y)²'],pts.map(v=>[v.x,v.y,fmt(a*v.x+b),fmt((a*v.x+b-v.y)**2)]))+`<p class="caption">Ajusta la recta para reducir MSE y después activa el outlier. Las cifras evalúan estos mismos ocho puntos, no una prueba de generalización.</p>`;};
 }else if(id==='gradient'){
  let a=0,b=0,iteration=0,history=[mse(demoPoints,0,0)],message='';
  controls.innerHTML=slider('rate','Learning rate · η',.001,.1,.001,.01)+`<div class="button-row"><button class="primary" id="one">Dar un paso</button><button class="secondary" id="ten">10 pasos</button><button class="secondary" id="reset">Reiniciar</button></div><p class="caption">Full batch sobre 8 puntos. El gradiente mantiene el factor 2/n. Una tasa alta puede divergir.</p>`;
  draw=()=>{const g=gradient(demoPoints,a,b);const max=Math.max(...history,1);out.innerHTML=svg(`<rect x="45" y="20" width="525" height="290" rx="8" fill="var(--plot)"/><path d="M45 20V310H570" stroke="var(--muted)" fill="none"/><polyline fill="none" stroke="#127e80" stroke-width="3" points="${history.map((v,i)=>`${45+i/Math.max(history.length-1,1)*525},${310-v/max*270}`).join(' ')}"/><text x="50" y="16">MSE · máximo ${fmt(max)}</text><text x="300" y="346" text-anchor="middle">Actualizaciones: 0 → ${iteration}</text>`,'Evolución de MSE con cada paso de gradiente')+`<div class="lab-stats">${stat('Paso',iteration)}${stat('MSE',fmt(history.at(-1)))}${stat('a / b',fmt(a)+' / '+fmt(b))}</div><div class="calculation">∂L/∂a = ${fmt(g[0])}<br>∂L/∂b = ${fmt(g[1])}<br>a siguiente = ${fmt(a)} − ${value('rate')} · (${fmt(g[0])})<br>b siguiente = ${fmt(b)} − ${value('rate')} · (${fmt(g[1])})</div><p role="status">${message||'Los dos gradientes se calculan antes de modificar a y b.'}</p>`;};
  const advance=n=>{for(let i=0;i<n;i++){const [na,nb]=step(demoPoints,a,b,value('rate'));const loss=mse(demoPoints,na,nb);if(!Number.isFinite(loss)||loss>1e12){message='Divergencia: se detuvo el cálculo antes de exceder el límite numérico. Reduce η y reinicia.';break;}a=na;b=nb;iteration++;history.push(loss);message=loss>history.at(-2)?'La pérdida aumentó. Observa la tasa de aprendizaje.':'';}draw();};controls.querySelector('#one').onclick=()=>advance(1);controls.querySelector('#ten').onclick=()=>advance(10);controls.querySelector('#reset').onclick=()=>{a=b=iteration=0;history=[mse(demoPoints,0,0)];message='';draw();};
 }else if(id==='matrix'){
  controls.innerHTML=slider('w1','Peso w₁',-4,4,1,2)+slider('w2','Peso w₂',-4,4,1,-1)+slider('mb','Intercepto b',-3,3,1,1);
  draw=()=>{const w1=value('w1'),w2=value('w2'),b=value('mb');out.innerHTML=`<div class="matrix-diagram"><div><small>X · 2 × 2</small><pre>┌ 1  2 ┐
└ 3  4 ┘</pre></div><strong>×</strong><div><small>w · 2 × 1</small><pre>┌ ${w1} ┐
└ ${w2} ┘</pre></div><strong>+</strong><div><small>b · 1</small><pre>┌ ${b} ┐
└ ${b} ┘</pre></div></div><h3>Fila por columna</h3><div class="calculation">ŷ₁ = 1 × (${w1}) + 2 × (${w2}) + (${b}) = <b>${w1+2*w2+b}</b><br>ŷ₂ = 3 × (${w1}) + 4 × (${w2}) + (${b}) = <b>${3*w1+4*w2+b}</b></div><p>Las dimensiones interiores coinciden: (2 × <strong>2</strong>)(<strong>2</strong> × 1) → 2 × 1.</p><p class="caption">El resultado tiene una predicción por fila. Este es el mismo cálculo que usa un modelo de regresión con dos características.</p>`;};
 }else if(id==='scaling'){
  controls.innerHTML=slider('newx','Nueva observación x',0,12,.5,8);
  draw=()=>{const mean=4,sigma=Math.sqrt(8/3),x=value('newx');out.innerHTML=`<h3>Aprender la escala de train</h3>${table(['Etapa','Cálculo','Resultado'],[['Media μ','(2 + 4 + 6) / 3',4],['Varianza','(4 + 0 + 4) / 3',fmt(8/3)],['Desviación σ','√(8/3)',fmt(sigma)]])}<div class="lab-stats">${[2,4,6].map(v=>stat('Train x = '+v,fmt((v-mean)/sigma))).join('')}</div><div class="calculation">z nuevo = (${x} − 4) / ${fmt(sigma)} = <strong>${fmt((x-mean)/sigma)}</strong></div><p>El dato nuevo cambia; μ y σ se mantienen. Reajustar el escalador con la nueva observación alteraría las coordenadas aprendidas.</p>`;};
 }else if(id==='perceptron'){
  controls.innerHTML=select('gate','Compuerta',[['AND','AND'],['OR','OR'],['XOR','XOR']])+slider('eta','Tasa de aprendizaje · η',.25,1,.25,1)+`<div class="button-row"><button class="primary" id="p-step">Procesar un punto</button><button class="secondary" id="p-epoch">Completar epoch</button><button class="secondary" id="p-reset">Reiniciar</button></div>`;
  const truth={AND:[0,0,0,1],OR:[0,1,1,1],XOR:[0,1,1,0]},xs=[[0,0],[0,1],[1,0],[1,1]];let w=[0,0],b=0,index=0,epoch=1,last=null,mistakes=0;
  const reset=()=>{w=[0,0];b=0;index=0;epoch=1;last=null;mistakes=0;};
  draw=()=>{const gate=controls.querySelector('#gate').value,ys=truth[gate],positions=[[175,270],[175,80],[425,270],[425,80]];const cells=xs.map((x,i)=>{const z=w[0]*x[0]+w[1]*x[1]+b,p=z>=0?1:0,[cx,cy]=positions[i];return `<circle cx="${cx}" cy="${cy}" r="${i===index?24:18}" fill="${ys[i]?'#ffbf3f':'#35c7e6'}" stroke="${p===ys[i]?'#ffffff':'#ff6b62'}" stroke-width="${p===ys[i]?3:7}"/><text x="${cx}" y="${cy+5}" text-anchor="middle" fill="#071524">${x.join('')}</text><text x="${cx}" y="${cy+42}" text-anchor="middle">y=${ys[i]} · ŷ=${p}</text>`;}).join('');out.innerHTML=svg(`<rect x="55" y="25" width="490" height="305" rx="8" fill="var(--plot)"/><path d="M120 295H500 M135 310V50" stroke="var(--muted)"/><text x="310" y="350" text-anchor="middle">x₁</text><text x="28" y="180" transform="rotate(-90 28 180)" text-anchor="middle">x₂</text>${cells}`,'Cuatro entradas de la compuerta y predicciones actuales')+`<div class="lab-stats">${stat('Epoch',epoch)}${stat('Pesos','['+w.map(fmt).join(', ')+']')}${stat('Bias',fmt(b))}${stat('Errores vistos',mistakes)}</div>${last?`<div class="calculation">x = (${last.x.join(', ')}) · y = ${last.y}<br>z = ${fmt(last.z)} · ŷ = ${last.prediction} · error = ${last.error}<br>w nuevo = [${w.map(fmt).join(', ')}] · b nuevo = ${fmt(b)}</div>`:'<p>Empieza en el punto resaltado. El borde rojo identifica predicciones incorrectas con los parámetros actuales.</p>'}<p class="caption">AND y OR son separables. En XOR, observa que las correcciones no consiguen dejar los cuatro puntos bien al mismo tiempo.</p>`;};
  const advance=()=>{const gate=controls.querySelector('#gate').value,x=xs[index],y=truth[gate][index],u=perceptronUpdate(w,b,x,y,value('eta'));w=u.w;b=u.b;last={...u,x,y};mistakes+=Number(u.error!==0);index++;if(index===xs.length){index=0;epoch++;}};
  controls.querySelector('#p-step').onclick=()=>{advance();draw();};controls.querySelector('#p-epoch').onclick=()=>{const remaining=xs.length-index||xs.length;for(let i=0;i<remaining;i++)advance();draw();};controls.querySelector('#p-reset').onclick=()=>{reset();draw();};controls.querySelector('#gate').onchange=()=>{reset();draw();};
 }else if(id==='feature-lift'){
  controls.innerHTML=slider('fx','Punto · x₁',-1,1,.05,.6)+slider('fy','Punto · x₂',-1,1,.05,.4)+slider('threshold','Umbral radial',.1,1.5,.05,.5);
  draw=()=>{const x=value('fx'),y=value('fy'),t=value('threshold'),r2=x*x+y*y,px=300+x*125,py=170-y*125;out.innerHTML=svg(`<rect x="35" y="20" width="350" height="300" rx="8" fill="var(--plot)"/><path d="M60 170H360 M210 35V305" stroke="var(--muted)"/><circle cx="210" cy="170" r="${125*Math.sqrt(t)}" fill="#35c7e622" stroke="#35c7e6" stroke-width="3"/><circle cx="${px-90}" cy="${py}" r="9" fill="#ffbf3f"/><text x="210" y="342" text-anchor="middle">Plano original: x₁²+x₂²=${fmt(t)}</text><rect x="420" y="45" width="115" height="250" rx="8" fill="var(--plot)"/><path d="M445 270V70" stroke="var(--muted)"/><path d="M435 ${270-Math.min(t,1.5)/1.5*200}H510" stroke="#35c7e6" stroke-width="3"/><circle cx="475" cy="${270-Math.min(r2,1.5)/1.5*200}" r="9" fill="#ffbf3f"/><text x="475" y="325" text-anchor="middle">φ₃</text>`,'Punto dentro o fuera de un círculo y su nueva coordenada radial')+`<div class="lab-stats">${stat('x₁²+x₂²',fmt(r2))}${stat('Umbral',fmt(t))}${stat('Clase',r2>=t?'1 · exterior':'0 · interior')}</div><div class="calculation">φ(${fmt(x)}, ${fmt(y)}) = (${fmt(x)}, ${fmt(y)}, <strong>${fmt(r2)}</strong>)<br>${fmt(r2)} ${r2>=t?'≥':'<'} ${fmt(t)}</div><p>La frontera circular del panel izquierdo se convierte en una comparación sobre una sola coordenada en el panel derecho.</p>`;};
 }else if(id==='multiclass'){
  controls.innerHTML=select('strategy','Estrategia',[['ovr','One-vs-rest · scores por clase'],['ovo','One-vs-one · scores por pareja']])+slider('s1','Puntuación 1',-1,1,.05,.3)+slider('s2','Puntuación 2',-1,1,.05,.6)+slider('s3','Puntuación 3',-1,1,.05,.8);
  draw=()=>{const mode=controls.querySelector('#strategy').value,s=[value('s1'),value('s2'),value('s3')],names=['Gato','Perro','Humano'];let rows,winner,explain;if(mode==='ovr'){const max=Math.max(...s),k=s.indexOf(max);winner=names[k];rows=names.map((n,i)=>[`${n} vs resto`,fmt(s[i]),i===k?'✓ gana':'']);explain='Cada modelo usa todo el dataset con una clase positiva. Gana la puntuación más alta.';}else{const pairs=[[0,1],[0,2],[1,2]],votes=[0,0,0];rows=pairs.map((pair,i)=>{const chosen=s[i]>=0?pair[0]:pair[1];votes[chosen]++;return [`${names[pair[0]]} vs ${names[pair[1]]}`,fmt(s[i]),`vota ${names[chosen]}`];});const max=Math.max(...votes);winner=names[votes.indexOf(max)];explain=`Votos: ${names.map((n,i)=>n+' '+votes[i]).join(' · ')}. Un score positivo vota por la primera clase de la pareja.`;}out.innerHTML=`<h3>Resultado: ${winner}</h3>${table(['Clasificador','Score','Decisión'],rows)}<div class="lab-stats">${stat('Estrategia',mode==='ovr'?'k modelos':'k(k−1)/2')}${stat('Clase elegida',winner)}</div><p>${explain}</p><p class="caption">Las puntuaciones son didácticas. Un empate requiere una regla explícita de desempate.</p>`;};
 }else if(id==='sigmoid'){
  controls.innerHTML=slider('sxv','Entrada x',-5,5,.1,1)+slider('sw','Peso w',-3,3,.1,1.5)+slider('sb','Bias b',-5,5,.1,0)+slider('sth','Umbral de decisión',.1,.9,.05,.5);
  draw=()=>{const x=value('sxv'),w=value('sw'),b=value('sb'),threshold=value('sth'),z=w*x+b,p=sigmoid(z);const pts=[];for(let q=-6;q<=6;q+=.1)pts.push(`${70+(q+6)/12*470},${300-sigmoid(q)*240}`);const zx=Math.max(70,Math.min(540,70+(z+6)/12*470)),zy=300-p*240;out.innerHTML=svg(`<rect x="45" y="25" width="520" height="300" rx="8" fill="var(--plot)"/><path d="M70 300H540 M70 300V50" stroke="var(--muted)" fill="none"/><path d="M70 ${300-threshold*240}H540" stroke="#ffbf3f" stroke-dasharray="7 6" fill="none"/><polyline points="${pts.join(' ')}" fill="none" stroke="#35c7e6" stroke-width="4"/><path d="M${zx} 300V${zy}H70" stroke="#ff6b62" stroke-dasharray="4 4" fill="none"/><circle cx="${zx}" cy="${zy}" r="8" fill="#ffbf3f"/><text x="305" y="348" text-anchor="middle">entrada neta z</text>`,'Curva sigmoide y punto seleccionado')+`<div class="lab-stats">${stat('z = wx+b',fmt(z))}${stat('σ(z)',fmt(p))}${stat('Decisión',p>threshold?'Clase 1':'Clase 0')}</div><div class="calculation">z = (${fmt(w)})(${fmt(x)}) + (${fmt(b)}) = ${fmt(z)}<br>p̂ = 1 / (1 + e<sup>−z</sup>) = <strong>${fmt(p)}</strong></div>`;};
 }else if(id==='logloss'){
  controls.innerHTML=select('ly','Etiqueta real y',[['1','1 · positiva'],['0','0 · negativa']])+slider('lp','Probabilidad predicha p̂',.01,.99,.01,.8);

 }else if(id==='regularization'){
  controls.innerHTML=select('rtype','Penalización',[['l2','L2 · peso²'],['l1','L1 · |peso|']])+slider('lambda','Fuerza λ',0,3,.05,.5)+slider('rw','Peso que quieres evaluar',-1,5,.05,2);

 }else{
  controls.innerHTML=slider('dim','Dimensión · d',2,1000,1,10)+slider('ratio','Radio interior / exterior',.5,.99,.01,.9);

text(await tools.apply_patch("*** Begin Patch\n*** Update File: D:\\GitHub\\ML NJIT\\web\\src\\math.js\n@@\n export function regularizedObjective(w,lambda,type='l2'){const data=(w-3)**2,penalty=lambda*(type==='l1'?Math.abs(w):w*w);return {data,penalty,total:data+penalty};}\n+export const hingeLoss=(signedScore)=>Math.max(0,1-signedScore);\n+export const rbfSimilarity=(a,b,gamma)=>Math.exp(-gamma*a.reduce((sum,v,i)=>sum+(v-b[i])**2,0));\n+export function impurity(labels,criterion='entropy'){\n+ if(!labels.length)return 0;\n+ const counts=new Map();labels.forEach(y=>counts.set(y,(counts.get(y)||0)+1));\n+ const probabilities=[...counts.values()].map(n=>n/labels.length);\n+ return criterion==='gini'?1-probabilities.reduce((s,p)=>s+p*p,0):-probabilities.reduce((s,p)=>s+p*Math.log2(p),0);\n+}\n+export function splitImpurity(rows,threshold,criterion='entropy'){\n+ const left=rows.filter(row=>row.x<=threshold),right=rows.filter(row=>row.x>threshold);\n+ const before=impurity(rows.map(row=>row.y),criterion);\n+ const after=(left.length*impurity(left.map(row=>row.y),criterion)+right.length*impurity(right.map(row=>row.y),criterion))/rows.length;\n+ return {left,right,before,after,gain:before-after};\n+}\n+export function forestMajority(votes){const counts=new Map();votes.forEach(y=>counts.set(y,(counts.get(y)||0)+1));const max=Math.max(...counts.values());return {counts:Object.fromEntries(counts),winners:[...counts].filter(([,n])=>n===max).map(([y])=>y)};}\n*** End Patch"));

  const rows=[{x:1,y:0},{x:2,y:0},{x:3,y:0},{x:4,y:1},{x:5,y:1},{x:6,y:1},{x:7,y:0},{x:8,y:1}];
  controls.innerHTML=select('tree-criterion','Criterio',[['entropy','Entropía · bits'],['gini','Gini']])+slider('tree-threshold','Pregunta: ¿x ≤ t?',1.5,7.5,1,3.5);
  draw=()=>{
   const criterion=controls.querySelector('#tree-criterion').value,t=value('tree-threshold'),s=splitImpurity(rows,t,criterion);
   const points=rows.map(p=>'<circle cx="'+(75+p.x*55)+'" cy="150" r="11" fill="'+(p.y?'#ffbf3f':'#35c7e6')+'"/>').join('');
   const scale=n=>fmt(n),pct=n=>n+'/'+rows.length;
   out.innerHTML=svg('<rect x="55" y="40" width="490" height="190" rx="8" fill="var(--plot)"/><path d="M75 150H525" stroke="var(--muted)"/><path d="M'+(75+t*55)+' 65V205" stroke="#ff6b62" stroke-width="3" stroke-dasharray="6 5"/>'+points+'<text x="300" y="265" text-anchor="middle">Azul: clase 0 · ámbar: clase 1 · coral: umbral</text>','Ocho ejemplos numéricos y el umbral de división')+
    '<div class="lab-stats">'+stat('Antes',scale(s.before))+stat('Izquierda · '+s.left.length,scale(s.left.length?s.left.length/rows.length*0+impurityFor(s.left,criterion):0))+stat('Derecha · '+s.right.length,scale(impurityFor(s.right,criterion)))+stat('Después',scale(s.after))+stat('Reducción',scale(s.gain))+'</div>'+
    '<div class="calculation">Paso 1: separa con x≤'+t+'. Paso 2: calcula la mezcla de cada lado.<br>Paso 3: '+pct(s.left.length)+'×'+scale(impurityFor(s.left,criterion))+' + '+pct(s.right.length)+'×'+scale(impurityFor(s.right,criterion))+' = <strong>'+scale(s.after)+'</strong>.<br>Paso 4: reducción = '+scale(s.before)+' − '+scale(s.after)+' = '+scale(s.gain)+'.</div>'+
    table(['Rama','x','Etiquetas'],[['Izquierda',s.left.map(p=>p.x).join(', '),s.left.map(p=>p.y).join(', ')],['Derecha',s.right.map(p=>p.x).join(', '),s.right.map(p=>p.y).join(', ')]])+
    '<p class="caption">Prueba todos los umbrales y busca la menor impureza posterior. Este ejemplo es de clasificación; en regresión se compararía el error de los valores numéricos.</p>';
  };
 }else if(id==='forest-vote'){
  const bootstrap=['1, 2, 2, 4','1, 3, 3, 4','2, 2, 3, 4','1, 1, 3, 4','1, 2, 4, 4'];

   out.innerHTML='<div class="lab-stats">'+stat('Votos clase 0',count0)+stat('Votos clase 1',count1)+stat('Resultado',result.winners.length===1?'Clase '+result.winners[0]:'Empate')+'</div>'+
    '<div class="calculation">Paso 1: cada árbol aprende con filas remuestreadas y características candidatas aleatorias.<br>Paso 2: cada árbol vota para el mismo ejemplo.<br>Paso 3: compara '+count0+' votos frente a '+count1+' y selecciona la mayoría.</div>'+
    table(['Árbol','Muestra ilustrativa con reemplazo','Voto'],votes.map((v,i)=>['T'+(i+1),bootstrap[i],'Clase '+v]))+
    '<p class="caption">Las muestras ilustran que una fila puede repetirse. Cambia los votos para explorar desacuerdo. Los árboles del ejemplo no están entrenados aquí; para regresión se promedian números en lugar de votar clases.</p>';
  };
 }else return false;
 controls.querySelectorAll('input,select').forEach(input=>input.addEventListener('input',()=>{const label=controls.querySelector('#'+input.id+'-value');if(label)label.textContent=input.value;draw();}));
 draw();return true;
}
function impurityFor(rows,criterion){const counts={};rows.forEach(p=>counts[p.y]=(counts[p.y]||0)+1);const probs=Object.values(counts).map(n=>n/rows.length);return criterion==='gini'?1-probs.reduce((s,p)=>s+p*p,0):-probs.reduce((s,p)=>s+p*Math.log2(p),0);}
`;
